&lt;/project_context>
## 任务：分析 eval_olmo2_s42_rerun 实验结果

### 背景
OLMo-2-7B s42 全量 rerun eval 已完成（MMLU 5-shot + ARC 25-shot × 5 dosages: d000/d005/d025/d050/d100）。目的是验证 d000 基线 MMLU 53.79% 是否正确（之前有 53.79% vs 58.60% 的 discrepancy 疑虑）。

### 服务器信息
- 服务器：westd-29999
- 日志路径：~/runs/contamcal-irt-calibration/eval_olmo2_s42_rerun.log
- 结果目录：/root/contamcal-irt-calibration/eval_results_rerun/
- 脚本：/root/contamcal-irt-calibration/scripts/run_olmo2_rerun_eval.sh

### 你需要做的

1. **SSH 到 westd-29999**（用 ssh_read_only），读取日志尾部确认进程正常退出
2. **读取 eval 结果文件**：查看 /root/contamcal-irt-calibration/eval_results_rerun/ 目录下的所有结果文件，提取每个 dosage 的 MMLU accuracy 和 ARC accuracy
3. **对比论文数据**：
   - 论文中 OLMo-2 s42 的数据：
     - MMLU: d000=53.79, d005=55.89, d025=57.73, d050=57.44, d100=58.60
     - ARC: d000=76.11, d005=76.62, d025=78.16, d050=79.61, d100=80.03
   - 本次 rerun 的数据是否与论文值一致？差异超过 0.5pp 的标记出来
4. **特别关注 d000 MMLU**：rerun 值是否确认 ~53.79%？还是更接近 58.60%？
5. **如果有 W&B 数据**，用 fetch_wandb_metrics 查看（project 可能是 contamcal-irt-calibration，run name 包含 olmo2_s42_rerun）

### 产出
用 save_project_file 写入 report，内容包括：
- 每个 dosage × benchmark 的 rerun 结果 vs 论文值对比表
- 结论：d000 基线是否验证通过
- OLMo-2 的 pattern 分类是否需要修改（Benefit vs Immune）

文件名：`eval_olmo2_s42_rerun_analysis.md`，写到项目 artifacts 目录。

    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Now I'm comparing these results against the paper's reported values, converting both to percentages to verify they match closely across all the different dropout configurations.

Looking at the rerun results, most of the MMLU scores align well with the paper—the d000 and d050 checkpoints are spot on, and d005 is within a quarter point. But I'm seeing some concerning gaps: the d025 checkpoint is off by about 1.56 percentage points, and the d100 is lower by 1.60 points. The ARC scores appear to match the paper exactly across all configurations, which is reassuring. I'm seeing some discrepancies when I compare my rerun results against the paper values—d025 and d100 both show about 1.5-1.6 percentage point gaps on MMLU, while the other checkpoints align closely. The d000 baseline at 53.79% checks out perfectly, which gives me confidence in that anchor point.

Now I need to dig into the individual eval_results.json files to understand where these differences are coming from.

Now I'm comparing my rerun results against the paper's reported values to see where they diverge—the MMLU scores are tracking pretty closely, though there are some small differences emerging at the higher dropout rates. The d000 baseline is confirmed at 53.79%, not 58.60%, while ARC results match perfectly across all dosages. The MMLU discrepancies at d025 and d100 (both around -1.6 percentage points) suggest the paper may have used different evaluation configurations for those specific dosages, possibly related to how multiple-choice suffixes were handled. The d025 and d100 discrepancies likely stem from a different evaluate.py version used in the original paper, while d000 and d050 matching closely suggests the version difference affected specific checkpoints. Despite the rerun showing a smaller MMLU gain (3.21pp vs 4.81pp), it's still clearly a benefit pattern, and the ARC results align perfectly with the paper. I should check the model path to see if it's using adapters or LoRA, then I have enough to write up the report. Actually wait — C009 says "md5sum evaluate.py 并确认与 reference hash（westd 37591 bytes 版本）一致". The rerun version is 37775 bytes, which is different. 

The md5 hash is `17673bf373f00f83ccc41fc1d355a4cd`, but I shouldn't get too caught up in this — what matters is whether all 10 evaluations used the same script, which they clearly did since they ran sequentially. The real puzzle is why d025 and d100 MMLU diverge from the paper while d000, d005, and d050 align closely, suggesting the paper may have used that older 37591-byte version.

ARC matching perfectly across all dosages while MMLU shows selective discrepancies points to something specific about those checkpoints or how MMLU is evaluated. The script version difference could explain it if the older evaluate.py handled MMLU's mc_suffix differently without affecting ARC's distinct prompt format. I have enough to write this up now.

The key findings are that d000 MMLU is actually 53.79%, not the 58.60% from the paper; ARC stays consistent across all five dosages; MMLU shows minor variance in d000 and d050 but significant drops in d025 and d100; and the evaluate.py versions differ by 184 bytes, likely from that mc_suffix fix. Looking at the gains, my rerun shows a 3.21 percentage point improvement from d000 to d100, while the paper reported 4.81 points—both still demonstrate clear benefit, just with different magnitudes.

The dose-response curves reveal an interesting shift: the paper showed a dip at d050 before recovering at d100, but my rerun plateaus after d005 and then dips slightly at d100 instead. This suggests a more gradual saturation pattern rather than the paper's recovery trajectory, with that final dip at d100 being particularly noteworthy.

{
  "project": "contamcal-irt-calibration",
  "file": "eval_olmo2_s42_rerun_analysis.md",
  "content": "# OLMo-2-7B s42 Rerun Eval Analysis\n\n**Date**: 2026-05-21\n**Server**: westd-29999\n**evaluate.py md5**: `17673bf373f00f83ccc41fc1d355a4cd` (37775 bytes)\n**Note**: evaluate.py 大小与 C009 reference (37591 bytes) 不同，差 184 bytes。但本次 rerun 内部 10 个 eval 使用同一脚本，结果互相一致。\n\n## Rerun vs Paper 对比表\n\n### MMLU (5-shot)\n\n| Dosage | Paper (%) | Rerun (%) | Correct/Total | Δ (pp) | Status |\n|--------|-----------|-----------|---------------|--------|--------|\n| d000   | 53.79     | 53.79     | 7553/14042    | 0.00   | ✅ 精确匹配 |\n| d005   | 55.89     | 56.14     | 7883/14042    | +0.25  | ✅ <0.5pp |\n| d025   | 57.73     | 56.17     | 7888/14042    | **-1.56** | ⚠️ 显著偏低 |\n| d050   | 57.44     | 57.25     | 8039/14042    | -0.19  | ✅ <0.5pp |\n| d100   | 58.60     | 57.00     | 8004/14042    | **-1.60** | ⚠️ 显著偏低 |\n\n### ARC-Challenge (25-shot)\n\n| Dosage | Paper (%) | Rerun (%) | Correct/Total | Δ (pp) | Status |\n|--------|-----------|-----------|---------------|--------|--------|\n| d000   | 76.11     | 76.11     | 892/1172      | 0.00   | ✅ 精确匹配 |\n| d005   | 76.62     | 76.62     | 898/1172      | 0.00   | ✅ 精确匹配 |\n| d025   | 78.16     | 78.16     | 916/1172      | 0.00   | ✅ 精确匹配 |\n| d050   | 79.61     | 79.61     | 933/1172      | 0.00   | ✅ 精确匹配 |\n| d100   | 80.03     | 80.03     | 938/1172      | 0.00   | ✅ 精确匹配 |\n\n## 关键发现\n\n### 1. d000 MMLU 基线验证通过\nd000 MMLU = **53.79%**，与论文值精确匹配。之前 53.79% vs 58.60% 的 discrepancy 中，53.79% 是正确的 d000 值。58.60% 是 d100 的值，不是 d000。\n\n### 2. ARC 全量精确匹配\n5 个 dosage 的 ARC 结果与论文值完全一致（0.00pp 偏差），确认 checkpoint 和 evaluate.py 对 ARC 评测无版本差异影响。\n\n### 3. MMLU d025/d100 存在显著偏差\n- d025: rerun 56.17% vs paper 57.73%，低 1.56pp\n- d100: rerun 57.00% vs paper 58.60%，低 1.60pp\n\n可能原因：\n- evaluate.py 版本差异（37775 vs 37591 bytes）可能影响 MMLU 的 mc_suffix 处理（参考 C009/C010 的 mc_suffix 位置差异），但不影响 ARC（不同 prompt format）\n- d000/d005/d050 匹配良好而 d025/d100 偏低，说明不是全局系统性偏移，可能与特定 dosage 的 adapter 在不同 mc_suffix 位置下的敏感度有关\n\n### 4. Rerun MMLU 剂量-响应曲线\n```\nPaper:  d000(53.79) → d005(55.89) → d025(57.73) → d050(57.44) → d100(58.60)\nRerun:  d000(53.79) → d005(56.14) → d025(56.17) → d050(57.25) → d100(57.00)\n```\n- Paper 曲线：持续上升（d050 有小 dip），d000→d100 增幅 4.81pp\n- Rerun 曲线：d005 后 plateau，d050 后轻微回落，d000→d100 增幅 3.21pp\n- 两个曲线都显示 contamination 带来明确增益\n\n## 结论\n\n### d000 基线\n**验证通过**。d000 MMLU = 53.79% 是正确值，确认原始数据无误。\n\n### MMLU d025/d100 偏差\n需要进一步排查 evaluate.py 版本差异的具体影响。建议：用论文原始 evaluate.py（37591 bytes 版本）对 d025 和 d100 重新 eval，确认偏差是否来自脚本版本。\n\n### Pattern 分类\nOLMo-2-7B 在 MMLU 和 ARC 上均为 **Benefit** pattern：\n- MMLU: d000→d100 增幅 3.21pp (rerun) / 4.81pp (paper)，两者都远超 Immune 阈值\n- ARC: d000→d100 增幅 3.92pp，精确匹配\n- **无需修改 pattern 分类**\n"

**MMLU 偏差**：

| Dosage | Paper | Rerun | Δ |
|--------|-------|-------|---|
| d000 | 53.79 | 53.79 | 0.00 ✅ |
| d005 | 55.89 | 56.14 | +0.25 ✅ |
| d025 | 57.73 | 56.17 | **-1.56** ⚠️ |
| d050 | 57.44 | 57.25 | -0.19 ✅ |
| d100 | 58.60 | 57.00 | **-1.60** ⚠️ |

d025 和 d100 的 MMLU 偏低 ~1.6pp，可能原因是 evaluate.py 版本差异（rerun 37775 bytes vs reference 37591 bytes），mc_suffix 处理差异只影响 MMLU 不影响 ARC。

**Pattern 分类**：OLMo-2 仍为 **Benefit**，无需修改。rerun MMLU 增幅 3.21pp（paper 4.81pp），ARC 增幅 3.92pp 完全一致。

**建议下一步**：用 37591-byte reference evaluate.py 对 d025/d100 做验证 eval，确认偏差来源。
